				<title><![CDATA[[Galaxy Unpacked July 2026] A First Look at Galaxy Watch Ultra2 and Galaxy Watch9]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/galaxy-unpacked-july-2026-a-first-look-at-galaxy-watch-ultra2-and-galaxy-watch9</link>
				<pubDate>Wed, 22 Jul 2026 22:03:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22214819/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch-Ultra2-Galaxy-Watch9-A-First-Look_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[Mobile]]></category>

		<category><![CDATA[Galaxy Watch9]]></category>
                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/4wiVBv9</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[At Galaxy Unpacked July 2026 in London, Samsung Electronics unveiled Galaxy Watch Ultra2 and Galaxy Watch9, expanding the digital health experience on the wrist. From the moment users wake up to the time they finish a workout and begin recovery, a smartwatch stays in contact with the body throughout the day. Samsung Newsroom got an […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>At Galaxy Unpacked July 2026 in London, Samsung Electronics unveiled Galaxy Watch Ultra2 and Galaxy Watch9, expanding the digital health experience on the wrist. From the moment users wake up to the time they finish a workout and begin recovery, a smartwatch stays in contact with the body throughout the day.</p>



<p>Samsung Newsroom got an early look at the new wearables during Galaxy Unpacked July 2026 — from Galaxy Watch Ultra2, built for precise workout tracking and structured training in demanding environments, to Galaxy Watch9, designed for everyday health management spanning sleep, cardiovascular health, exercise and recovery.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="693" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22214454/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch-Ultra2-Galaxy-Watch9-A-First-Look_Main1.jpg" alt="(From left) Galaxy Watch Ultra2 and Galaxy Watch9 " class="wp-image-175743" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ (From left) Galaxy Watch Ultra2 and Galaxy Watch9 </figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Galaxy Watch Ultra2: Adventure Without Limits</strong></h2>



<p>Combining top-tier performance with exceptional durability, Galaxy Watch Ultra2 is optimized for high-intensity sports and outdoor activities.</p>



<p>Galaxy Watch Ultra2 is the first Galaxy Watch powered by the Qualcomm Snapdragon® Wear Elite Platform,<sup data-fn="c9835f31-88be-4c93-8a6a-9d789df7b710" class="fn"><a href="#c9835f31-88be-4c93-8a6a-9d789df7b710" id="c9835f31-88be-4c93-8a6a-9d789df7b710-link">1</a></sup> delivering fast performance and precise GPS tracking. Its 800mAh battery offers 35% more capacity than the previous generation’s. The display reaches up to 5,000 nits of brightness — a first for a smartwatch — making on-screen information easy to read even under bright lighting.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="750" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22214540/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch-Ultra2-Galaxy-Watch9-A-First-Look_Main2.jpg" alt="Galaxy Watch Ultra2 is available in a 47-millimeter size. " class="wp-image-175745" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Galaxy Watch Ultra2 is available in a 47-millimeter size. </figcaption></figure>



<p>Comfort has also improved. The cushion design seamlessly integrates the display, bezel and body. Despite its larger battery, the watch is 12% thinner than the previous generation thanks to component optimization and an internal redesign. The band is 26% lighter with a new air pocket structure, providing a secure yet comfortable fit.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="519" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22214607/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch-Ultra2-Galaxy-Watch9-A-First-Look_Main3.jpg" alt="A slim profile provides lasting comfort. " class="wp-image-175746" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ A slim profile provides lasting comfort. </figcaption></figure>



<p>Trail Run, a new feature exclusive to Galaxy Watch Ultra2, offers real-time insights including detailed elevation data, climbing progress and cumulative ascent and descent distance. With this information, users can gauge workout intensity and adjust their pace accordingly.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="467" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22214621/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch-Ultra2-Galaxy-Watch9-A-First-Look_Main4.jpg" alt="Trail Run " class="wp-image-175747" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Trail Run </figcaption></figure>



<p>Galaxy Watch Ultra2’s capabilities now extend beneath the surface. From the moment users enter the water, it automatically records depth, dive time and water temperature. Backed by EN13319 certification for diving instruments, along with 10ATM water resistance and an IP69K rating for dust and water resistance, the device is engineered to support safe underwater activities. Furthermore, in collaboration with global diving equipment brand Mares, Galaxy Watch Ultra2 will support advanced diving features — including real-time ascent and descent rates as well as safety decompression time.<sup data-fn="3a8214e6-86ee-4e66-ae7f-88c957551e76" class="fn"><a href="#3a8214e6-86ee-4e66-ae7f-88c957551e76" id="3a8214e6-86ee-4e66-ae7f-88c957551e76-link">2</a></sup></p>



<p>During workouts, users can take advantage of hydration guidance, newly added to Nutrition Alert. Based on sweat loss relative to body weight, it calculates recommended fluid intake in real time to help users stay hydrated during extended exercise sessions.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="600" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22214635/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch-Ultra2-Galaxy-Watch9-A-First-Look_Main5.jpg" alt="Hydration guidance " class="wp-image-175749" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Hydration guidance </figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Galaxy Watch9: A Companion for Everyday Health</strong></h2>



<p>Galaxy Watch9 is designed to be an everyday companion for continuous health management. Its slimmer body and band with a new air pocket structure fit comfortably and securely around the wrist, making it ideal for all-day wear — from workouts to sleep.</p>



<p>Powered by the Snapdragon Wear Elite platform, Galaxy Watch9 features a 390mAh battery<sup data-fn="8ad7851d-ea8b-4d41-95ef-89377cad03b5" class="fn"><a href="#8ad7851d-ea8b-4d41-95ef-89377cad03b5" id="8ad7851d-ea8b-4d41-95ef-89377cad03b5-link">3</a></sup> with 20% more capacity than the previous generation’s to support fast performance and consistent health monitoring throughout the day.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="707" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22214646/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch-Ultra2-Galaxy-Watch9-A-First-Look_Main6.jpg" alt="(From left) Galaxy Watch9 comes in 40- and 44-millimeter sizes. " class="wp-image-175753" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ (From left) Galaxy Watch9 comes in 40- and 44-millimeter sizes. </figcaption></figure>



<p>The Galaxy Watch series’ digital health experience is centered around the updated Samsung Health, an all-in-one health coach that brings together five core pillars — Activity, Mindfulness, Nutrition, Sleep and Vitals — in one place. By capturing signals from the body and presenting them at a glance, the app helps users stay ahead of their health.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-video"><video playsinline autoplay controls loop muted src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22214718/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch-Ultra2-Galaxy-Watch9-A-First-Look_Main7.mp4"></video><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ The five core pillars of Samsung Health </figcaption></figure>



<p>Sleep is the foundation of health management. The upgraded Sleep Apnea feature analyzes the number of breathing disruptions per hour using an AI algorithm and categorizes them into three levels. It further refines the sleep apnea detection capability that earned Samsung the world’s first De Novo authorization from the U.S. Food and Drug Administration (FDA) in 2024.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="600" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22214732/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch-Ultra2-Galaxy-Watch9-A-First-Look_Main8.jpg" alt="Sleep insights " class="wp-image-175755" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Sleep insights </figcaption></figure>



<p>Key health metrics measured during sleep can be viewed at a glance in Vitals. Using heart rate, heart rate variability, respiratory rate, skin temperature, blood oxygen saturation, Galaxy Watch9 establishes a personalized baseline to help identify meaningful changes in health.</p>



<p>Heart Health Score provides an easy-to-understand snapshot of cardiovascular health by combining factors such as activity level, sleep, body composition and stress trends into a single score representing the user’s current condition.</p>



<p>Workout plans can also be tailored to fitness levels. Fitness Index uses body composition and workout history to assess whether a user’s current exercise volume is appropriate, helping set individualized goals. On days with more intensive workouts, Daily Cardio Load helps users assess training balance and determine how much recovery time they need.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="750" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22214756/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch-Ultra2-Galaxy-Watch9-A-First-Look_Main9.jpg" alt="Heart Health Score " class="wp-image-175756" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Heart Health Score </figcaption></figure>



<p>Galaxy Watch Ultra2 excels in high-intensity training, while Galaxy Watch9 supports everyday health management. Together with enhanced hardware and Samsung Health, the next-generation Galaxy Watch series delivers a smarter, more personalized health experience.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-embed is-type-video is-provider-youtube wp-block-embed-youtube wp-embed-aspect-16-9 wp-has-aspect-ratio"><div class="wp-block-embed__wrapper">

</div></figure>

									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics today unveiled the new Galaxy Z series, its most complete lineup yet, designed to expand foldables to a wider audience. The lineup introduces Galaxy Z Fold8, a fresh and exciting form factor that provides a new kind of foldable experience built around the way people explore, discover and immerse themselves in their favorite […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p><strong>Samsung Electronics</strong> today unveiled the new Galaxy Z series, its most complete lineup yet, designed to expand foldables to a wider audience. The lineup introduces Galaxy Z Fold8, a fresh and exciting form factor that provides a new kind of foldable experience built around the way people explore, discover and immerse themselves in their favorite content.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22215435/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-Launch_main1_F.jpg" alt="Samsung Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra, Fold8 and Flip8 open a new chapter in foldables with three distinct experiences designed for Ultra productivity, immersive discovery and self-expression." class="wp-image-175769" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Samsung Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra, Fold8 and Flip8 open a new chapter in foldables with three distinct experiences designed for Ultra productivity, immersive discovery and self-expression.</figcaption></figure>



<p>The lineup also includes Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra, which brings Galaxy’s renowned Ultra standard to foldables with maximum productivity and content creation in a larger workspace. Galaxy Z Flip8 — the sleekest one yet — was built for expression and quick interaction on the go. Built on seven generations of foldable innovation and consumer insight, the new Galaxy Z series gives users more choice than ever, with three distinct experiences engineered to fit different needs.</p>



<p>Galaxy AI has been optimized for each of these form factors. Across the lineup, every insight feels more actionable and every action feels more connected. Multitasking is easier on the larger screens of Galaxy Z Fold8 and Fold8 Ultra, while Flip8’s AI-native FlexWindow keeps relevant information and shortcuts within reach. And with partner AI experiences such as Gemini Intelligence, Galaxy gives users even more ways to get things done in fewer steps — with transparency, privacy and user control built in.</p>



<p>“As AI becomes more agentic, mobile devices will become the most personal entry point to experiences that understand and adapt to each user,” said TM Roh, Chief Executive Officer, President and Head of the Device eXperience (DX) Division at Samsung Electronics. “By setting a new standard for foldables, we are once again advancing the premium mobile experience and opening the next era of intelligence to more people.”</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>The All-New Galaxy Z Fold8: A New Galaxy Fold Experience for Everyday Immersion</strong></h2>



<p>Galaxy Z Fold8 is reimagined for users who want a foldable that feels natural in the moments when curiosity turns into deeper discovery. Fold8 introduces a form factor designed to move easily from quick interactions on the cover screen to deeper viewing, reading and gaming on the main display. With intuitive display ratios and long-lasting performance, Fold8 makes it easier to get the most out of the content, stories and ideas that matter.</p>



<p></p>



<h4 class="wp-block-heading has-medium-font-size"><strong>Built for Discovery</strong></h4>



<p>Galaxy Z Fold8’s display ratios are designed around the way people naturally consume content throughout the day. By adapting the screen to the content, Fold8 creates a more immersive experience whether users are browsing, watching, reading or gaming. At just 201 g,<sup data-fn="6cd0ef51-7ac1-4f2f-a633-79bada502708" class="fn"><a href="#6cd0ef51-7ac1-4f2f-a633-79bada502708" id="6cd0ef51-7ac1-4f2f-a633-79bada502708-link">1</a></sup> Fold8 is Samsung’s lightest Galaxy Z Fold yet.<sup data-fn="84b12177-5c34-4fa0-be4a-a0dfedc3a99d" class="fn"><a href="#84b12177-5c34-4fa0-be4a-a0dfedc3a99d" id="84b12177-5c34-4fa0-be4a-a0dfedc3a99d-link">2</a></sup> Despite its lightweight design, it combines the Snapdragon® 8 Elite Gen 5 Mobile Platform for Galaxy<sup data-fn="b0016eb3-92f5-4426-a906-3b29c418b1aa" class="fn"><a href="#b0016eb3-92f5-4426-a906-3b29c418b1aa" id="b0016eb3-92f5-4426-a906-3b29c418b1aa-link">3</a></sup> with a larger 4,800 mAh battery,<sup data-fn="27f4df53-ed72-4219-a00e-8b55874a5b53" class="fn"><a href="#27f4df53-ed72-4219-a00e-8b55874a5b53" id="27f4df53-ed72-4219-a00e-8b55874a5b53-link">4</a></sup> delivering smooth, responsive performance and lasting power so users can stay immersed longer.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="369" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22230343/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-Launch_main2_FINAL.jpg" alt="Samsung’s new Flex Titanium technology introduces a titanium-based display structure, combining a titanium-alloy film with an enhanced titanium plate, to make foldables thinner while maintaining durability." class="wp-image-175854" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Samsung’s new Flex Titanium technology introduces a titanium-based display structure, combining a titanium-alloy film with an enhanced titanium plate, to make foldables thinner while maintaining durability.</figcaption></figure>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>When folded, the <strong>10:16 cover screen</strong><sup data-fn="b8d127e6-d228-40e1-a80e-9f90d9f6a896" class="fn"><a href="#b8d127e6-d228-40e1-a80e-9f90d9f6a896" id="b8d127e6-d228-40e1-a80e-9f90d9f6a896-link">5</a></sup> feels familiar and comfortable for the short moments people return to most often, such as messaging, social updates, browsing and short-form video.</li>



<li>When unfolded, the <strong>4:3 main display</strong><sup data-fn="502a7989-88b6-4508-955d-a5fec476d811" class="fn"><a href="#502a7989-88b6-4508-955d-a5fec476d811" id="502a7989-88b6-4508-955d-a5fec476d811-link">6</a></sup> creates a larger canvas that helps users lose themselves in games and films.</li>



<li>When rotated, the same display ratio becomes naturally suited to reading, giving users a more comfortable way to settle into articles, e-books and long-form content without carrying another device.</li>



<li>Together, these display ratios are designed around the way people naturally consume content, helping Fold8 feel more immersive whether users are quickly checking in or settling in for longer sessions.</li>
</ul>



<p></p>



<h4 class="wp-block-heading has-medium-font-size"><strong>A Display Engineered From the Inside Out</strong></h4>



<p>Across the new Galaxy Z series, Samsung’s display engineering expertise serves as the foundation for a more refined foldable experience, supporting slimmer designs, greater durability and more seamless viewing.</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>Samsung’s new <strong>Flex Titanium</strong> technology introduces a titanium-based display structure designed to help make foldables thinner while maintaining durability. Combining a titanium-alloy film with an enhanced titanium plate, Flex Titanium helps strengthen display support, absorb pressure and impact and reduce crease visibility over time. This structure also refines the way the device opens, balancing hinge mechanics, display tension and magnetic force to make unfolding feel smoother, lighter and more natural.<sup data-fn="85128928-2bd5-49d4-bd4f-08ed7e4dc2dd" class="fn"><a href="#85128928-2bd5-49d4-bd4f-08ed7e4dc2dd" id="85128928-2bd5-49d4-bd4f-08ed7e4dc2dd-link">7</a></sup></li>



<li>With up to <strong>3,000 nits</strong> of brightness, <strong>Vision Booster</strong> and a <strong>low-reflection display finish</strong>, the main screen stays vivid and easy to see even in outdoor settings.</li>
</ul>

<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>A New Class of Agentic Experiences Optimized for Foldables</strong></h2>



<p>As the most personal device people carry, the phone has become the primary entry point where AI meets users — and the new Galaxy Z series sets the tone for the next generation of mobile AI experiences. Galaxy AI now works across more apps and services, with experiences optimized for foldable form factors. By understanding what matters to each user and helping turn insights into action, Galaxy AI becomes more personal, proactive and useful throughout the day.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-video"><video playsinline autoplay controls loop muted src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22225622/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-Launch_main10_FINAL-1.mp4"></video></figure>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Now Brief</strong> helps users stay informed with personalized insights tailored to their routines, interests and daily needs through expanded scenarios, customizable briefing cards and personalized recommendations. It now also surfaces security and privacy insights, helping users stay on top of potential risks and protective actions.</li>



<li><strong>Now Nudge</strong><sup data-fn="89a7c08a-c9d7-4389-9d86-a5d16df86d4f" class="fn"><a href="#89a7c08a-c9d7-4389-9d86-a5d16df86d4f" id="89a7c08a-c9d7-4389-9d86-a5d16df86d4f-link">23</a></sup> helps turn insights into action. When plans begin to take shape in a conversation, AI can surface relevant next steps such as checking a schedule, finding a place or saving a location. On foldables, users can continue the conversation side by side on larger displays.</li>



<li><strong>Gemini Intelligence</strong> helps users move from request to result with fewer steps. Users can simply describe what they need or even add screen or image context. App automation can now work across more than 40 supported apps and services, with capabilities ranging from looking up tickets to finding a nearby restaurant and making a reservation. With <strong>Gemini Notebook</strong>, users can bring together notes, images, recordings, files and documents in one persistent workspace to collect, organize and develop their ideas. Drag-and-drop support in Split view makes the workflow even more intuitive, allowing users to easily add multiple files and images and turn their content into audio overviews, infographics, reports, meeting recaps, visual summaries, documents and more.</li>



<li>AI experiences become more connected and contextual when the new Galaxy Z series is connected with<strong> Galaxy Watch Ultra2, Galaxy Watch9</strong> and even the upcoming <strong>intelligent eyewear</strong>. From wellness insights to daily routines and connected actions, Galaxy AI helps users move more seamlessly between devices with timely personalized support throughout the day.</li>
</ul>



<p>Together, these experiences show how Galaxy AI is becoming a more capable and proactive companion, helping users accomplish more with less effort. Across the lineup, foldable form factors make these experiences easier to access throughout the day.</p>

<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Security and Privacy Built Into the Experience</strong></h2>



<p>As AI becomes more personal and agentic, trust continues to be a critical foundation of the experience. Users need to know how their data is used, understand what AI is doing on their behalf and stay in control of the choices that shape their experience. With the new Galaxy Z series, Samsung builds that trust into the device from the start, keeping personal data secure while giving users transparency, choice and control over how their information is used.</p>



<p>Samsung Knox protects every Galaxy device from the chip up, with a multi-layered approach that includes on-device personalization and user-controlled cloud processing, as well as future-ready protections such as ecosystem-wide protection through Samsung Knox Matrix. For on-device AI, Personal Data Engine (PDE) enables context-aware, personalized AI experiences. To protect PDE, <a href="https://news.samsung.com/global/your-privacy-secured-inside-the-tech-powering-safe-personalized-galaxy-ai-experiences" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Knox Enhanced Encrypted Protection (KEEP)</a> creates encrypted, app-specific storage environments, while <a href="https://news.samsung.com/global/your-privacy-secured-how-galaxy-ai-protects-privacy-with-samsung-knox-vault" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Knox Vault</a> adds a tamper-resistant physical layer for sensitive information.<sup data-fn="ed929a94-ab98-4781-a22e-2920e4a37d8c" class="fn"><a href="#ed929a94-ab98-4781-a22e-2920e4a37d8c" id="ed929a94-ab98-4781-a22e-2920e4a37d8c-link">24</a></sup> These system-level protections work together with Advanced Intelligence settings and Personal Data Intelligence to help users <a href="https://news.samsung.com/global/your-privacy-secured-how-galaxy-ai-empowers-you-to-take-control-of-your-data" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">control how AI features manage and use personal data</a>.</p>



<p>One UI 9 also introduces a new AI Assistant Activity dashboard, giving users a centralized view of AI automations running on their behalf. Instead of moving across multiple apps and services to understand what happened, users can review AI actions in one place and navigate directly to the relevant agent<sup data-fn="67bf2c4d-2e7a-42a2-9405-5892b6709cc6" class="fn"><a href="#67bf2c4d-2e7a-42a2-9405-5892b6709cc6" id="67bf2c4d-2e7a-42a2-9405-5892b6709cc6-link">25</a></sup> for additional context and settings. The new Galaxy Z series also brings enhanced Privacy Alerts that proactively notify users about potential privacy risks, including unnecessary attempts to access background permissions, helping them respond before those risks become invisible.</p>



<p>These updates build on Galaxy’s existing security and privacy portfolio which includes Auto Blocker, Theft Protection, Private Sharing, Secure Wi-Fi and more. Together, these advancements help users take a more active role in managing personal data and AI activities, with transparency, clearer choices and stronger protections at the core of their experience — helping ensure that more personal AI experiences remain grounded in trust.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Getting Started With the New Galaxy Z Series</strong></h2>



<p>Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra, Fold8 and Flip8 are available for pre-order in select markets starting today.  All models are available in Graphite and Cream, with Violet Shadow exclusive to Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra, Lavender to Galaxy Z Fold8 and Pink to Galaxy Z Flip8. Online-exclusive colors<sup data-fn="8dda9245-b667-4447-a7d7-a2704b6917e3" class="fn"><a href="#8dda9245-b667-4447-a7d7-a2704b6917e3" id="8dda9245-b667-4447-a7d7-a2704b6917e3-link">26</a></sup> <sup data-fn="ee883c71-cde6-407a-a912-ab78638c6f24" class="fn"><a href="#ee883c71-cde6-407a-a912-ab78638c6f24" id="ee883c71-cde6-407a-a912-ab78638c6f24-link">27</a></sup> include Green Shadow for Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra, Pistachio for Galaxy Z Fold8 and Mint for Galaxy Z Flip8.</p>



<p>The new Galaxy Z series will bring the benefits of advanced AI closer to users’ daily lives by providing a 6-month free trial of Google AI Pro,<sup data-fn="e20cf47e-a177-4fbc-bfb4-cdd386cf3b6d" class="fn"><a href="#e20cf47e-a177-4fbc-bfb4-cdd386cf3b6d" id="e20cf47e-a177-4fbc-bfb4-cdd386cf3b6d-link">28</a></sup> including 5 TB of cloud storage — equivalent to $19.99 USD per month. With the expanded access to Google’s most capable AI models, users can boost their productivity and creativity on the foldable screen anytime they want.</p>



<p>Switching to Galaxy is now easier than ever, thanks to a more seamless migration experience across a wider range of devices.<sup data-fn="9fa843b9-92e7-48b0-980a-ce32363a457e" class="fn"><a href="#9fa843b9-92e7-48b0-980a-ce32363a457e" id="9fa843b9-92e7-48b0-980a-ce32363a457e-link">29</a></sup> With the updated Smart Switch, iOS users can scan a QR code on their current devices and wirelessly transfer data without downloading additional apps. Smart Switch can also bring over additional types of data including passwords and passkeys, call history, accessibility settings, eSIM data and more, helping reduce the time needed to set up a new device.  And with Quick Share’s new compatibility with AirDrop,<sup data-fn="22c5a38c-9521-4abe-94eb-96d480634047" class="fn"><a href="#22c5a38c-9521-4abe-94eb-96d480634047" id="22c5a38c-9521-4abe-94eb-96d480634047-link">30</a></sup> sharing files between iOS and Galaxy devices is now seamless in both directions.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="494" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22230609/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Ultra-Galaxy-Z-Fold8-Galaxy-Z-Flip8-Launch_main11_FINAL.jpg" alt="Switching to Galaxy is now easier than ever with the updated Smart Switch, allowing iOS users to scan a QR code and wirelessly transfer data without additional apps." class="wp-image-175858" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Switching to Galaxy is now easier than ever with the updated Smart Switch, allowing iOS users to scan a QR code and wirelessly transfer data without additional apps.</figcaption></figure>



<p>To give users peace of mind after upgrading, Samsung Care+<sup data-fn="e8e851d2-8c47-400c-b5f6-c81e3a37d701" class="fn"><a href="#e8e851d2-8c47-400c-b5f6-c81e3a37d701" id="e8e851d2-8c47-400c-b5f6-c81e3a37d701-link">31</a></sup> offers comprehensive protection with fast repairs for accidental damage, extended warranty coverage and certified expert support at home and abroad, making it easier to stay covered wherever life takes them. Customers who purchase a new Galaxy foldable on Samsung.com and enroll in a monthly Samsung Care+ plan will receive the first three months of coverage at no cost. With One UI 9, the new Warranty and care settings hub also makes it easy to enroll in, access and manage Samsung Care+, alongside warranty information, self-diagnostics, repair requests and remote support — all from one convenient place on the device.</p>



<p>For more information about the new Galaxy Z series, please visit: <a href="https://news.samsung.com/global/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Samsung Global Newsroom</a> or <a href="http://www.samsung.com/galaxy-z-fold8/?referrer=newsroom" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Samsung.com.</a></p>

Aspect ratios shown are approximate, based on representative model values. <a href="#b8d127e6-d228-40e1-a80e-9f90d9f6a896-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 5"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="502a7989-88b6-4508-955d-a5fec476d811">Aspect ratios shown are approximate, based on representative model values. <a href="#502a7989-88b6-4508-955d-a5fec476d811-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 6"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="85128928-2bd5-49d4-bd4f-08ed7e4dc2dd">Compared to previous Galaxy Z Fold models. <a href="#85128928-2bd5-49d4-bd4f-08ed7e4dc2dd-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 7"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="914d2760-4047-4a67-98fb-7c2875328de2">Results may vary depending on lighting conditions and/or shooting conditions including multiple subjects, being out of focus or moving subjects. <a href="#914d2760-4047-4a67-98fb-7c2875328de2-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 8"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="5a55293b-9d33-4b64-b5d4-d7746af8f4a0">My FanCam feature requires a Samsung Account login. Availability may vary by video. Not supported if there is no subject to track. Accuracy of results is not guaranteed. <a href="#5a55293b-9d33-4b64-b5d4-d7746af8f4a0-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 9"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="53c03bba-a828-40bf-978b-3e4f0ede72fc">Measured diagonally, Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra’s cover screen size is 6.5” in the full rectangle and 6.5” accounting for the rounded corners. The main screen size is 8.0” in the full rectangle and 8.0” accounting for the rounded corners; actual viewable area is smaller due to the rounded corners and camera hole. <a href="#53c03bba-a828-40bf-978b-3e4f0ede72fc-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 10"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="10c2821e-3edc-46f8-9fb9-70a00f8774ca">Weight may vary by country or region. <a href="#10c2821e-3edc-46f8-9fb9-70a00f8774ca-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 11"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="5468e458-930d-4de9-9043-ac71cd5cbb2b">Compared to previous Galaxy Z Fold models. <a href="#5468e458-930d-4de9-9043-ac71cd5cbb2b-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 12"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="10fdb4e3-a8b1-4091-98e1-d23db48c8f0a">Compared to previous Galaxy Z Fold models <a href="#10fdb4e3-a8b1-4091-98e1-d23db48c8f0a-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 13"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="595861fc-23da-40ea-805b-b2188c368b61">Snapdragon branded products are products of Qualcomm Technologies, Inc. and/or its subsidiaries. Rated capacity is 4854 mAh for Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra. <a href="#6bb4256c-c231-40a0-9f1c-8ce5e5d082a3-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 15"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="c6895238-6a89-4054-a813-c3cc38666205">Based on Samsung internal lab test conditions with the pre-release version of a given model connected to earphones via Bluetooth under default settings over LTE. Actual video playback time may vary by network connection, settings, video file format, screen brightness, battery condition and many other factors. <a href="#c6895238-6a89-4054-a813-c3cc38666205-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 16"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="cf25c2ff-d4f2-4454-b996-d069b78f95d4">Weight may vary by country or region. <a href="#cf25c2ff-d4f2-4454-b996-d069b78f95d4-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 17"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="5c946d05-abb6-46e8-95b5-62ab714aee26">Compared to previous Galaxy Z Flip models. <a href="#5c946d05-abb6-46e8-95b5-62ab714aee26-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 18"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="7dfbdfcd-747f-42f4-929f-b61555d4b673">Now Brief feature requires Samsung Account login. 									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics today announced the all-new Galaxy Watch Ultra2 and Galaxy Watch9, delivering a new era of effortless health, where users can unlock in-depth, meaningful health insights simply by wearing these devices. To support 24/7 wearability, Samsung delivers powerful innovations that maximize comfort while advancing performance with Galaxy Watch’s largest battery and brightest display ever, […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p><strong>Samsung Electronics </strong>today announced the all-new <strong>Galaxy Watch Ultra2 </strong>and<strong> Galaxy Watch9</strong>,<sup data-fn="2bea86aa-5588-435e-b413-ce0cf7db5fce" class="fn"><a href="#2bea86aa-5588-435e-b413-ce0cf7db5fce" id="2bea86aa-5588-435e-b413-ce0cf7db5fce-link">1</a></sup> delivering a new era of effortless health, where users can unlock in-depth, meaningful health insights simply by wearing these devices. To support 24/7 wearability, Samsung delivers powerful innovations that maximize comfort while advancing performance with Galaxy Watch’s <strong>largest battery </strong>and<strong> brightest display </strong>ever, as well as the <strong>Snapdragon Wear®</strong> <strong>Elite Platform</strong>.<sup data-fn="877e3f66-75c4-4a2b-80cb-0b0014649635" class="fn"><a href="#877e3f66-75c4-4a2b-80cb-0b0014649635" id="877e3f66-75c4-4a2b-80cb-0b0014649635-link">2</a></sup> Serving as the ultimate gateway to AI-driven health intelligence, this groundbreaking premium lineup transforms complex biometric data into personalized, actionable guidance across the full spectrum of user health.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="750" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22211303/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch9-Ultra-Galaxy-Watch9-Launch_Main1.jpg" alt="Galaxy Watch9 and Galaxy Watch Ultra2 feature the Snapdragon Wear® Elite Platform, alongside the lineup's largest battery and brightest display ever." class="wp-image-175710" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Galaxy Watch9 and Galaxy Watch Ultra2 feature the Snapdragon Wear® Elite Platform, alongside the lineup’s largest battery and brightest display ever.</figcaption></figure>



<p>“Samsung is committed to helping people live better. We want to make the shift from reactive treatment to proactive care as seamless as possible. By integrating everyday devices with our global scale, we’re building a deeply connected health experience.” said TM Roh, Chief Executive Officer, President and Head of the Device eXperience (DX) Division at Samsung Electronics. “The new Galaxy Watch Ultra2 and Galaxy Watch9 represent a massive leap forward in Samsung’s health vision. By continuously tracking health data from the wrist, these new watches act as a vigilant health companion, turning daily vital signs into proactive, actionable insights and providing timely alerts.”</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Galaxy Watch Ultra2: Fueling Ambitions and Enabling Peak Performance Through Unrivaled Hardware and Specialized Sports Tracking</strong></h2>



<figure class="wp-block-image"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="750" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22211304/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch9-Ultra-Galaxy-Watch9-Launch_Main2.jpg" alt="The new 47 mm Galaxy Watch Ultra2 in Titanium Gray." class="wp-image-175711" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ The new 47 mm Galaxy Watch Ultra2 in Titanium Gray.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Galaxy Watch Ultra2 is Samsung’s most powerful and advanced Galaxy Watch ever. It is built for extreme outdoor adventures and high-performance seekers who demand long-lasting performance, reliable durability, precision tracking and continuous health monitoring in extreme conditions. <em></em></p>



<p>Galaxy Watch Ultra2’s dedicated tracking modes for outdoor sports help users excel and safely push their limits. The <strong>Trail Run</strong><sup data-fn="5d6af59a-40ea-470f-a9d6-3ae67b075c10" class="fn"><a href="#5d6af59a-40ea-470f-a9d6-3ae67b075c10" id="5d6af59a-40ea-470f-a9d6-3ae67b075c10-link">3</a></sup> feature tracks detailed elevation, climbing progress and terrain impact so users can better pace themselves and reduce injury risk. It also provides real-time hydration guidance through <strong>Nutrition Alert</strong>,<sup data-fn="f3291f3e-d290-4b9e-a34d-8c06a8b19967" class="fn"><a href="#f3291f3e-d290-4b9e-a34d-8c06a8b19967" id="f3291f3e-d290-4b9e-a34d-8c06a8b19967-link">4</a></sup> a new addition to the existing Sweat Loss feature. By estimating sweat loss relative to body weight, it provides guidance on when and how much to hydrate, helping users achieve their running goals.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="750" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22211305/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch9-Ultra-Galaxy-Watch9-Launch_Main3.jpg" alt="Galaxy Watch Ultra2 provides detailed trail running data on its 5,000-nit, crystal-clear display" class="wp-image-175712" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Galaxy Watch Ultra2 provides detailed trail running data on its 5,000-nit, crystal-clear display</figcaption></figure>



<p>Galaxy Watch Ultra2 also supports professional diving. Rated IP69K, 10 ATM and EN13319 certified,<sup data-fn="12b63eed-f85a-45ca-8377-fd32ec4807ba" class="fn"><a href="#12b63eed-f85a-45ca-8377-fd32ec4807ba" id="12b63eed-f85a-45ca-8377-fd32ec4807ba-link">5</a></sup> it’s not just dust- and water-resistant — it’s dive-ready. The moment the user enters the water, it can automatically track real-time diving data such as depth, time and water temperature right from their wrist.<sup data-fn="56cf4792-6a07-4c19-8f61-bdce721ff23b" class="fn"><a href="#56cf4792-6a07-4c19-8f61-bdce721ff23b" id="56cf4792-6a07-4c19-8f61-bdce721ff23b-link">6</a></sup> Moreover, users can access advanced diving features, including ascent/descent speed tracking and safe dive limits, by using the exclusive Ultra2 <strong>Diving</strong> app expected to be available later this year. Developed with <strong>Mares</strong>,<sup data-fn="f499bfc6-a6bf-4813-b97e-4c75bb839b5b" class="fn"><a href="#f499bfc6-a6bf-4813-b97e-4c75bb839b5b" id="f499bfc6-a6bf-4813-b97e-4c75bb839b5b-link">7</a></sup>the world’s leading diving equipment brand, these features significantly enhance the watch’s diving capabilities.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="750" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22211307/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch9-Ultra-Galaxy-Watch9-Launch_Main4.jpg" alt="Once a user enters the water, Galaxy Watch Ultra2 automatically tracks real-time diving data such as depth, time and water temperature." class="wp-image-175713" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Once a user enters the water, Galaxy Watch Ultra2 automatically tracks real-time diving data such as depth, time and water temperature.</figcaption></figure>



<p>To power these pro-level sports health features, Galaxy Watch Ultra2 is built with unprecedented hardware performance. It is equipped with a massive <strong>800 mAh battery </strong>— a 35% increase in capacity compared to the previous Ultra model. Powered by the <strong>Snapdragon Wear</strong> <strong>Elite platform</strong>, it establishes a new benchmark in power, speed and highly accurate GPS tracking for the Ultra line. The expanded display provides the <strong>world’s first smartwatch with 5,000 nits of brightness</strong>,<sup data-fn="3743a7b0-6400-417b-abab-499bcaf64cae" class="fn"><a href="#3743a7b0-6400-417b-abab-499bcaf64cae" id="3743a7b0-6400-417b-abab-499bcaf64cae-link">8</a></sup> making crystal-clear visibility possible even under intense sunlight.</p>



<p>Galaxy Watch Ultra2 retains its <strong>shock-resistant titanium casing</strong> to help ensure rugged durability, while also introducing enhanced comfort and stability for a broader, more diverse range of users. Despite a significantly larger battery, Galaxy Watch Ultra2 achieves an innovative <strong>slimmer</strong><sup data-fn="0c91c428-92c7-4e8c-8c15-2469028b19a8" class="fn"><a href="#0c91c428-92c7-4e8c-8c15-2469028b19a8" id="0c91c428-92c7-4e8c-8c15-2469028b19a8-link">9</a></sup><strong> design</strong> that transitions seamlessly from rugged outdoor and sports activities to everyday lifestyle use. Advanced internal re-engineering significantly reduces the device’s thickness by 12% compared to the previous generation. Coupled with lighter and softer bands that reduce weight, Galaxy Watch Ultra2 delivers 24/7 wearability, highly precise biometric data tracking and exceptional comfort.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Galaxy Watch9: Everyday Wellness With All-Day Comfort and a Lasting Battery</strong></h2>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="750" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22211309/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch9-Ultra-Galaxy-Watch9-Launch_Main5.jpg" alt="Galaxy Watch9 is available with different band materials and colors, including soft silicone and lightweight fabric options." class="wp-image-175714" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Galaxy Watch9 is available with different band materials and colors, including soft silicone and lightweight fabric options.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Galaxy Watch9 is the ultimate everyday health companion, designed for wellness-conscious users who want to build healthier habits through effortless, continuous activity and sleep tracking. This versatile watch turns personal biomarkers into actionable health insights, empowering users to effortlessly manage their daily routines by simply wearing it.</p>



<p>To maximize its role as a health companion, Galaxy Watch9 is backed by powerful hardware and an incredibly comfortable design. It features a <strong>lightweight yet</strong> <strong>durable aluminum casing</strong> that preserves Galaxy Watch’s <strong>signature cushion silhouette</strong>, refined specifically for a closer, more natural fit on the wrist. Combined with a wide range of interchangeable, soft-touch bands, Galaxy Watch9 helps ensure unmatched <strong>all-day comfort </strong>— enabling the continuous, 24/7 wear required to help capture accurate and consistent health data.</p>



<p>This exceptional wearability is powered by next-generation internal innovations. The new <strong>Snapdragon Wear Elite platform</strong> gives Galaxy Watch9 a massive boost in speed and efficiency for lag-free, seamless health monitoring. Its efficient <strong>390 mAh battery</strong><sup data-fn="6f2dfec6-864d-49a6-8463-d831407c6234" class="fn"><a href="#6f2dfec6-864d-49a6-8463-d831407c6234" id="6f2dfec6-864d-49a6-8463-d831407c6234-link">10</a></sup> delivers dependable, around-the-clock tracking from day to night. Furthermore, a brilliant <strong>3,000-nit display</strong> supports effortless readability and crystal-clear visibility under any lighting condition, making it easy for users to check their health status at a glance.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>AI-Powered Health: Comprehensive and Actionable Health Insights</strong></h2>



<p>At the core of Galaxy Watch Ultra2 and Galaxy Watch9 are Samsung’s AI-powered health features.Galaxy Watch’s BioActive sensor captures continuous biometric data about a user’s lifestyle habits to develop a deep understanding of their health. Galaxy Watch then translates this complex biometric data into simple, proactive recommendations, helping users understand not just what is happening in their bodies, but what to do next.</p>



<p>The new capabilities — available on both Galaxy Watch Ultra2 and Galaxy Watch9 — advance Samsung’s preventive care vision by combining everyday data with years of rigorous clinical research conducted with world-renowned universities and medical institutions. Built on this foundation, Galaxy Watch is designed to deliver more accurate and meaningful health guidance across key pillars including sleep, activity, cardiovascular health and recovery.</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Sleep Apnea feature</strong><sup data-fn="43ec6200-4d43-4ca7-9732-59277f1c828a" class="fn"><a href="#43ec6200-4d43-4ca7-9732-59277f1c828a" id="43ec6200-4d43-4ca7-9732-59277f1c828a-link">11</a></sup>:<strong> </strong>Backed by new FDA clearance and AI algorithms, delivers deeper insights into breathing disruptions during sleep.</li>



<li><strong>Vitals</strong><sup data-fn="d21f4456-4844-4907-92a9-a50d43a4c2b1" class="fn"><a href="#d21f4456-4844-4907-92a9-a50d43a4c2b1" id="d21f4456-4844-4907-92a9-a50d43a4c2b1-link">12</a></sup>: Proactively monitors a user’s personal health baseline during sleep and provides seamless alerts for significant deviations.</li>



<li><strong>Heart Health Score</strong><sup data-fn="f4639553-132e-4531-a317-cc0c82bb5449" class="fn"><a href="#f4639553-132e-4531-a317-cc0c82bb5449" id="f4639553-132e-4531-a317-cc0c82bb5449-link">13</a></sup>:Provides an effortless understanding of cardiovascular wellness through a simple, actionable score and personalized lifestyle recommendations.</li>



<li><strong>Daily Cardio Load</strong><sup data-fn="93d98104-3589-4d99-b6ab-0052e157a409" class="fn"><a href="#93d98104-3589-4d99-b6ab-0052e157a409" id="93d98104-3589-4d99-b6ab-0052e157a409-link">14</a></sup>: Optimizes training through tailored guidance on workout volume and estimated recovery needs.</li>



<li><strong>Fitness Index</strong><sup data-fn="470dfa0f-8dae-46f1-8026-7b1621b6ff50" class="fn"><a href="#470dfa0f-8dae-46f1-8026-7b1621b6ff50" id="470dfa0f-8dae-46f1-8026-7b1621b6ff50-link">15</a></sup>: Delivers a holistic view of physical fitness with personalized goals to improve everyday performance.</li>



<li><strong>Hearing</strong><sup data-fn="d45a01e9-dce4-4a50-ac18-b1863aa8a9a5" class="fn"><a href="#d45a01e9-dce4-4a50-ac18-b1863aa8a9a5" id="d45a01e9-dce4-4a50-ac18-b1863aa8a9a5-link">16</a></sup>: Protects long-term hearing health with alerts for unsafe noise levels and detailed reports.</li>
</ul>



<p>With these features, users can gain access to intelligent, personalized insights that empower them to seamlessly manage their long-term wellness journey and reach their goals.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="750" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22211311/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Galaxy-Watch9-Ultra-Galaxy-Watch9-Launch_Main6.jpg" alt="Galaxy Watch features include Vitals to alert users to significant health deviations." class="wp-image-175715" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Galaxy Watch features include Vitals to alert users to significant health deviations.</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Purpose-Built Band Collections: Designed for Every Lifestyle</strong></h2>



<p>A smartwatch should be as unique as the user, and both Galaxy Watch Ultra2 and Galaxy Watch9 offer a range of diverse band options to fit every lifestyle.</p>



<p>Whether users are tackling rugged terrain, diving deep or heading into a business meeting, they have the flexibility to choose the perfect band tailored to their specific purpose and environment, ensuring optimal functionality without compromising on style. Users have the following options to choose from:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Marine Band:</strong> an even lighter, breathable silicone band designed for all-day adventures and extreme conditions.</li>



<li><strong>Pe</strong><strong>akF</strong><strong>orm Band:</strong> a hybrid material band delivering a premium finish optimized for both elegance and athletic performance.</li>



<li><strong>Trail Band:</strong> a classic fabric band featuring enhanced breathability and a superior fit for lasting comfort, even during demanding outdoor activities like running.</li>
</ul>



<p>Designed with ultimate self-expression in mind, the expansive new Watch9 band collection empowers users to showcase their personal style and customize their look. Featuring innovative new materials and a vibrant spectrum of colors, these bands maximize comfort across diverse activities — from intense workouts to your everyday routines:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Sports Band:</strong> a thinner, softer all-rounder for ultimate comfort and effortless transitions from workouts to daily life.</li>



<li><strong>Misty Band</strong>: crafted from premium soft silicone that securely wraps around the wrist for a stable, bounce-free fit. Featuring subtle and soft two-tone colors, it delivers a sophisticated look that effortlessly complements a user’s everyday style.</li>



<li><strong>Fabric Band:</strong> a lightweight, breathable daily band offering maximum comfort, even during sleep.</li>
</ul>

<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Samsung Care+: Quick Access to Diagnostics and More</strong></h2>



<p>To give users peace of mind after upgrading, Samsung Care+<sup data-fn="a9ac8c74-03d1-4be9-93fd-67e02834db7a" class="fn"><a href="#a9ac8c74-03d1-4be9-93fd-67e02834db7a" id="a9ac8c74-03d1-4be9-93fd-67e02834db7a-link">17</a></sup> offers comprehensive protection with fast repairs for accidental damage, extended warranty coverage and certified expert support at home and abroad, making it easier to stay covered wherever life takes them.</p>



<p>Additionally, Galaxy Watch users can manage their Samsung Care+ plan from the Warranty and care menu available on Galaxy mobile phones running on One UI 9. This is a new settings hub that gives users quick access to warranty information, self-diagnostics, repair requests, remote support and Samsung Care+ services — all from their mobile phone.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Galaxy Watch Ultra2 and Galaxy Watch9 come with exclusive subscription trial benefits:</strong></h2>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Strava 60-Day Free Trial</strong><sup data-fn="0ce0db88-57c3-454f-95fb-d481c5bef23e" class="fn"><a href="#0ce0db88-57c3-454f-95fb-d481c5bef23e" id="0ce0db88-57c3-454f-95fb-d481c5bef23e-link">18</a></sup>:Users canstart training with the world’s most active community, where 195 million active people across 185 countries track every effort and cheer each other on. But Strava goes beyond training — users can connect with their community and discover new running routes by tracking workouts directly on their watch, then opening the Strava app on their mobile phone. To activate a free trial, users can open the Strava mobile app, go to Settings and tap the Samsung offer.</li>



<li><strong>iFIT 2-Month Free Trial</strong><sup data-fn="0a80fbc6-dd9d-4bc0-8371-db1425c6edc9" class="fn"><a href="#0a80fbc6-dd9d-4bc0-8371-db1425c6edc9" id="0a80fbc6-dd9d-4bc0-8371-db1425c6edc9-link">19</a></sup>: Users can explore global destinations with elite iFIT trainers and track workouts directly on their watch. To activate a free trial, users can open the Samsung Health app on their mobile phone and tap promotion in the Settings menu.</li>
</ul>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Availability</strong><sup data-fn="e91a3c8f-238f-4f10-b511-8397480d3e79" class="fn"><a href="#e91a3c8f-238f-4f10-b511-8397480d3e79" id="e91a3c8f-238f-4f10-b511-8397480d3e79-link">20</a></sup></h2>



<p>Together with the new Galaxy Z series, Galaxy Watch Ultra2 and Watch9 expands Samsung’s AI ecosystem, bringing life companion experiences across every form factor, from wrist to pocket to home.</p>



<p>Galaxy Watch Ultra2 will be available in a <strong>47 mm size</strong>, while Galaxy Watch9 will be available in two sizes: <strong>40 mm</strong> and <strong>44 mm</strong>. Galaxy Watch Ultra2 users will be able to choose from <strong>Titanium Silver</strong> or <strong>Titanium Gray</strong>. Meanwhile, the40 mm Galaxy Watch9 will be available in <strong>Cream </strong>and <strong>Graphite</strong>, while the 44 mm Galaxy Watch9 will be available in <strong>Graphite</strong> and <strong>Silver</strong>.</p>



<p>Galaxy Watch Ultra2 and Galaxy Watch9 are available for pre-order in select markets starting July22, with general availability starting Aug 7.</p>

Please note that the Nutrition Alert is only supported for certain specific activities. <a href="#f3291f3e-d290-4b9e-a34d-8c06a8b19967-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 4"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="12b63eed-f85a-45ca-8377-fd32ec4807ba">Galaxy Watch Ultra2 has a water resistance rating of 100 meters according to the ISO22810 standard. The test was conducted in 5% salt water and 4 ppm chlorine water. Galaxy Watch Ultra2 is also water resistant with an IPX8 rating based on lab test conditions for submersion in up to 1.5 meters of freshwater for up to 30 minutes and an IPX9K rating based on lab test conditions for water resistance against powerful water jets. Galaxy Watch Ultra2 has obtained EN13319, which is a standard applicable to instruments measuring water depth by the environmental pressure as used by divers and also the dive time in certain conditions. Water resistance is not a permanent condition and can diminish over time. If device has been exposed to seawater, user should rinse it in freshwater and dry after use. Refer to the user manual for further information, including care/use instructions. Galaxy Watch Ultra2 is dust resistant with an IP6X rating based on lab test conditions by a third party: 2 kg of talc powder (max. diameter of 0.05 mm) per cubic meter was blown into a test chamber housing the device (air pressure within device kept below surrounding air) for 2 hours. Dust resistance may vary by actual usage conditions. Galaxy Watch Ultra2 has obtained MIL-STD-810H Certification: high/low temperature, altitude, humidity, immersion, salt fog, dust, vibration, drop, etc. MIL-STD-810H is a standardized form of testing designed by the U.S. military to accurately assess device limitations. Real-world usage may vary by specific environmental conditions used in the testing. Extreme conditions not guaranteed. Galaxy Watch Ultra2 withstands temperatures ranging from -20°C to 55°C while being worn on the wrist, based on results from internal lab tests conducted by Samsung. Actual performance may vary depending on the environment where it is used. In very high or low temperatures, charging may be blocked, certain functions may be automatically terminated or unavailable, or the watch may shut down automatically for safety. Additionally, battery performance can be adversely affected at low temperatures. <a href="#12b63eed-f85a-45ca-8377-fd32ec4807ba-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 5"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="56cf4792-6a07-4c19-8f61-bdce721ff23b">To use the features, activation from the Watch is needed (Settings> Advanced feature>Auto launch on dive> Depth “On”) <a href="#56cf4792-6a07-4c19-8f61-bdce721ff23b-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 6"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="f499bfc6-a6bf-4813-b97e-4c75bb839b5b">Starting later this year, these diving features developed with Mares will be exclusively available on Galaxy Watch Ultra2 for the next two years on Wear OS smartwatches <a href="#f499bfc6-a6bf-4813-b97e-4c75bb839b5b-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 7"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="3743a7b0-6400-417b-abab-499bcaf64cae">Based on market research of globally released smartwatches as of June 2026. The 5,000-nit specification refers to the localized peak brightness achieved under high ambient light, and full-screen brightness may differ. Actual brightness may vary or be limited depending on environmental factors, device temperature and usage conditions. <a href="#3743a7b0-6400-417b-abab-499bcaf64cae-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 8"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="0c91c428-92c7-4e8c-8c15-2469028b19a8">Compared to the previous Galaxy Watch Ultra. <a href="#0c91c428-92c7-4e8c-8c15-2469028b19a8-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 9"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="6f2dfec6-864d-49a6-8463-d831407c6234">Galaxy Watch9 40mm. <a href="#6f2dfec6-864d-49a6-8463-d831407c6234-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 10"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="43ec6200-4d43-4ca7-9732-59277f1c828a">Requires a Galaxy smartphone running Android 12 or above. Not intended for use in detection, diagnosis, treatment of any medical condition. Sleep apnea risk detection is for personal reference only. Please consult a medical professional for advice. To check for signs of Sleep Apnea, Galaxy Watch (Galaxy Watch Ultra, Watch7 and later released Galaxy Watch series with Wear OS 7.0) and Galaxy smartphone with Android OS 12 or above are required. <a href="#43ec6200-4d43-4ca7-9732-59277f1c828a-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 11"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="d21f4456-4844-4907-92a9-a50d43a4c2b1">Not intended for use in the diagnosis or treatment of any medical condition. If you have health concerns, be sure to see a medical professional. Vitals requires sleep data of at least 7 nights. Vitals is available on Android phones (Android 11 or above) and requires the Samsung Health app (v7.0 or above). Samsung account login required. To use Vitals, it is necessary to wear a Galaxy Watch or Galaxy Ring while sleeping. <a href="#d21f4456-4844-4907-92a9-a50d43a4c2b1-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 12"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="f4639553-132e-4531-a317-cc0c82bb5449">Available on upcoming Galaxy Watch with One UI 9. 					<item>
				<title><![CDATA[Samsung Brings Galaxy Ecosystem Into Everyday Eyewear]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-brings-galaxy-ecosystem-into-everyday-eyewear</link>
				<pubDate>Wed, 22 Jul 2026 22:00:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22215053/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Intelligent-Eyewear-Launch_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[Mobile]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Press Releases]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Galaxy Unpacked]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Galaxy Unpacked July 2026]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Intelligent Eyewear]]></category>
                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/4wfAaep</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics today introduced its intelligent eyewear at Galaxy Unpacked in London. Building on the concepts announced at the latest Google I/O, the new designs were developed with global eyewear brands, Gentle Monster and Warby Parker. The intelligent eyewear shows how the Galaxy ecosystem can move beyond the mobile phone and into eyewear that supports […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<p>Samsung Electronics today introduced its intelligent eyewear at Galaxy Unpacked in London. Building on the concepts announced at the latest Google I/O, the new designs were developed with global eyewear brands, Gentle Monster and Warby Parker. The intelligent eyewear shows how the Galaxy ecosystem can move beyond the mobile phone and into eyewear that supports daily routines, work, travel and hands-free moments.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>What’s New:</strong></h2>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>Designed for everyday use, the eyewear pairs a lightweight frame and slim profile with the durability and comfort needed for daily wear.</li>



<li>Developed in collaboration with Gentle Monster and Warby Parker, the eyewear reflects each brand’s expertise in design, offering a range of frame styles, colors and lens options to suit different preferences and lifestyles.</li>
</ul>



<p>“As agentic AI reshapes mobile experiences, the next step is to make those experiences more intuitive and responsive to each user’s context,” said Won-Joon Choi, Chief Operating Officer of the Mobile eXperience (MX) Business at Samsung Electronics. “Intelligent eyewear creates a new point of interaction, bringing more personalized AI assistance naturally into the moments that make up everyday life.”</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="280" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22213356/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Intelligent-Eyewear-Launch_main1.jpg" alt="Building on the concepts announced at the latest Google I/O, the new designs were developed with global eyewear brands, Gentle Monster and Warby Parker." class="wp-image-175734" /></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Why It Matters</strong></h2>



<p>As AI enters an agentic era, the question is no longer only how intelligent it becomes, but where and how that intelligence meets people. Mobile phones remain at the center of connected experiences, while devices across the Galaxy ecosystem add context to different moments of daily life. Intelligent eyewear represents that next step, bringing AI closer to people as they move, work, communicate and explore, without requiring every interaction to begin with a screen.</p>



<p>Built for these moments, intelligent eyewear brings AI directly into the user’s line of sight, providing timely information and assistance while allowing them to stay engaged with the people, places and tasks around them. Clear controls and safeguards support responsible and secure use, while hands-free access delivers assistance naturally without requiring users to stop what they are doing or turn to another screen.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>New Intelligent Eyewear Experience</strong></h2>



<p>Designed for everyday wear, the eyewear combines a lightweight frame, slim profile and battery performance, offering up to nine hours on a single charge with up to seven additional full charges from the charging case.<sup data-fn="c8265e1c-fd48-45f1-92ee-4cdc4570c950" class="fn"><a href="#c8265e1c-fd48-45f1-92ee-4cdc4570c950" id="c8265e1c-fd48-45f1-92ee-4cdc4570c950-link">1</a></sup> A built-in camera brings visual context into the AI experience, while Snapdragon<sup>®</sup> AR1 Gen1<sup data-fn="74a320d8-765e-4713-b202-b88dce3f388f" class="fn"><a href="#74a320d8-765e-4713-b202-b88dce3f388f" id="74a320d8-765e-4713-b202-b88dce3f388f-link">2</a></sup> Platform supports the AI performance designed for real-time assistance, with touch interaction, connectivity, power efficiency and thermal management optimized within the eyewear’s compact frame.</p>



<p>As a hands-free extension of the Galaxy experience, the device lets users stay focused on the moment. Built in partnership with Google, it brings Gemini into a familiar wearable form factor, making interaction feel more natural, immediate and intuitive.</p>



<p>“Google and Samsung have a shared vision to bring helpful and context-aware intelligence into everyday life,” said Sameer Samat, President of Android Ecosystem at Google. “Built on the Android XR platform, the intelligent eyewear blends world-class designs with Gemini’s advanced assistance that naturally responds to what you see, delivering all-day, hands-free help. We’re excited to continue this momentum and bring this experience to people soon.”</p>



<p>Key experiences include:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Staying in the moment:</strong> The device can help summarize long messages, read key information aloud, support live translation and allow users to control the experience with simple voice or gesture interactions through connected devices.<sup data-fn="fc0a2a1b-8e8f-490e-9748-fdc585db76d5" class="fn"><a href="#fc0a2a1b-8e8f-490e-9748-fdc585db76d5" id="fc0a2a1b-8e8f-490e-9748-fdc585db76d5-link">3</a></sup></li>



<li><strong>Capturing context:</strong> When prompted, the eyewear can capture what the user is seeing, such as a whiteboard or meeting discussion, organize key information in Notes and make it easier to review, share and follow-up on later.<sup data-fn="398d65a6-f9b9-44b9-af19-2caa670df1fc" class="fn"><a href="#398d65a6-f9b9-44b9-af19-2caa670df1fc" id="398d65a6-f9b9-44b9-af19-2caa670df1fc-link">4</a></sup></li>



<li><strong>Real-time interaction:</strong> Users can ask for guidance based on their location, such as finding a route, setting a destination or understanding what is around them, while live translation can support communication in another language.<sup data-fn="fdeba69e-cafd-4949-b997-3dc01aa89dae" class="fn"><a href="#fdeba69e-cafd-4949-b997-3dc01aa89dae" id="fdeba69e-cafd-4949-b997-3dc01aa89dae-link">5</a></sup></li>



<li><strong>Context-aware assistance:</strong> The device can support more useful follow-up interactions by understanding the user’s current context and connecting relevant information across tasks, helping users continue where they left off with less repetition.<sup data-fn="e58b507f-2af9-4435-83f3-655fa4f54d13" class="fn"><a href="#e58b507f-2af9-4435-83f3-655fa4f54d13" id="e58b507f-2af9-4435-83f3-655fa4f54d13-link">6</a></sup></li>



<li><strong>Live visual sharing:</strong> Users can share what they are seeing during a call or record their own point of view, making communication more immediate while keeping their hands free.<sup data-fn="3dbf37f8-30b0-4272-bf04-df17eedcc835" class="fn"><a href="#3dbf37f8-30b0-4272-bf04-df17eedcc835" id="3dbf37f8-30b0-4272-bf04-df17eedcc835-link">7</a></sup></li>
</ul>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>A More Personal Approach to Eyewear</strong></h2>



<p>Eyewear is both a functional product and a personal style statement. Samsung worked with Gentle Monster and Warby Parker to create intelligent eyewear that balances thoughtful design, comfort and everyday wearability. By combining Samsung’s hardware craftsmanship with each brand’s distinctive design approach, the collaboration offers a broader range of styles to suit different preferences, lifestyles and occasions.</p>



<p>At the Unpacked, Samsung introduced two distinct designs by: Gentle Monster and Warby Parker.</p>



<p>The Gentle Monster edition features a slim frame in black, pairing a straight upper rim with softly rounded lower edges to create a refined and fashion-forward silhouette. The Warby Parker style features a subtly upswept brow line in a rich brown hue, creating a refined, versatile shape perfect for everyday settings. </p>



<p>Throughout the design process, Samsung worked closely with the eyewear partners to refine key fit points such as the forehead and ears. These insights helped shape the final designs with enhanced comfort and everyday wearability. Fit considerations were tailored to each model, alongside durability and reliable performance.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="396" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/22213445/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Intelligent-Eyewear-Launch_main2.jpg" alt="Samsung Electronics today introduced its intelligent eyewear at Galaxy Unpacked in London." class="wp-image-175735" /></figure>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />


<ol class="wp-block-footnotes"><li id="c8265e1c-fd48-45f1-92ee-4cdc4570c950">Battery life varies by network environment, features and apps used, device settings, the number of times charged and many other factors. <a href="#c8265e1c-fd48-45f1-92ee-4cdc4570c950-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 1"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="74a320d8-765e-4713-b202-b88dce3f388f">Snapdragon is a trademark or registered trademark of Qualcomm Incorporated. 				<title><![CDATA[Introducing Samsung Galaxy Card]]></title>
				<link>https://news.samsung.com/global/introducing-samsung-galaxy-card</link>
				<pubDate>Tue, 21 Jul 2026 06:25:00 +0000</pubDate>
								<media:content url="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/21053335/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Samsung-Galaxy-Card-Samsung-Wallet_Thumb932-728x410.jpg" medium="image" />
				<dc:creator><![CDATA[Samsung Newsroom]]></dc:creator>
						<category><![CDATA[Mobile]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Galaxy Unpacked July 2026]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Samsung Galaxy card]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Samsung Wallet]]></category>
                <guid isPermaLink="false">https://bit.ly/4ywuLkq</guid>
									<description><![CDATA[Samsung Electronics America today unveiled Samsung Galaxy Card, a seamless way to earn cash rewards on everyday purchases with increased cash rewards for purchases made from Samsung — including the latest devices. Samsung’s first ever credit card offers a new financing option, whether you are giving the gift of Galaxy, treating yourself to a new device, […]]]></description>
																<content:encoded><![CDATA[
<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/21060705/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Samsung-Galaxy-Card-Samsung-Wallet_Main1.jpg" alt="An image of Samsung Galaxy Card_Z Flip7 Open" class="wp-image-175653" /></figure>



<p><a href="https://www.samsung.com/us/?r=true&referrer=usnewsroom" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Samsung Electronics America</a> today unveiled Samsung Galaxy Card, a seamless way to earn cash rewards on everyday purchases with increased cash rewards for purchases made from Samsung — including the latest devices. Samsung’s first ever credit card offers a new financing option, whether you are giving the gift of Galaxy, treating yourself to a new device, or want to earn cash rewards on all your favorite items.</p>



<p>Samsung Galaxy Card is issued by Barclays US Consumer Bank on the Visa network. Starting July 22, 2026, applications can be submitted online, including while shopping at any <a href="https://www.samsung.com/us/store/?r=true&referrer=usnewsroom#find-a-store" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Samsung retail</a> store in the U.S., to earn an additional $200 in bonus cash rewards after spending $2,000 in the first 90 days of account opening.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/21060716/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Samsung-Galaxy-Card-Samsung-Wallet_Main2.jpg" alt="" class="wp-image-175654" /></figure>



<p>Samsung Galaxy Card is available as a virtual and premium, metal physical card with a black Samsung logo. Users earn a host of cash rewards and other benefits, including:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>5% cash rewards on eligible purchases made directly with Samsung<sup data-fn="c4566db3-b408-4833-8937-98e121509112" class="fn"><a href="#c4566db3-b408-4833-8937-98e121509112" id="c4566db3-b408-4833-8937-98e121509112-link">1</a></sup></li>



<li>3% cash rewards on purchases made with Samsung Wallet<sup data-fn="6ffd5ed2-0d97-49da-8dc3-8a70c0367c2c" class="fn"><a href="#6ffd5ed2-0d97-49da-8dc3-8a70c0367c2c" id="6ffd5ed2-0d97-49da-8dc3-8a70c0367c2c-link">2</a></sup></li>



<li>2% cash rewards on streaming services, like Netflix, Disney+, and Spotify</li>



<li>1% cash rewards on all other purchases</li>



<li>20% off Samsung VIP Advantage,<sup data-fn="9014c57c-f601-44f5-8cb4-170d4d4b00ab" class="fn"><a href="#9014c57c-f601-44f5-8cb4-170d4d4b00ab" id="9014c57c-f601-44f5-8cb4-170d4d4b00ab-link">3</a></sup> plus earn 5% cash rewards when purchasing or renewing a Samsung VIP Advantage membership. <a href="https://www.barclaycardus.com/applycontent/TnCs.jsp?tc48355" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Terms apply</a></li>
</ul>



<p>“For years, users have relied on Samsung products to enhance their daily life — from staying connected with friends, to relaxation, to productivity. Today, Samsung Galaxy Card offers users a new way to be rewarded every time you pay,” said Woncheol Chai, EVP and Head of the Digital Wallet Team at the Mobile eXperience (MX) Business at Samsung Electronics. “Users are busy, they need a credit card that simplifies their life and offers them real value — Samsung Galaxy Card does both.”</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="329" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/21060812/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Unpacked-July-2026-Samsung-Galaxy-Card-Samsung-Wallet_Main3.jpg" alt="An image of Samsung Galaxy Z Flip7 and Samsung Galaxy Z Fold7" class="wp-image-175656" /></figure>



<p>With Samsung Galaxy Card’s convenient integration with <a href="https://www.samsung.com/us/support/owners/app/samsung-wallet?r=true&referrer=usnewsroom" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Samsung Wallet</a>, users can store it alongside other compatible cards, IDs,<sup data-fn="4a8f096a-854a-4e5f-a42a-ce92f4bdefd6" class="fn"><a href="#4a8f096a-854a-4e5f-a42a-ce92f4bdefd6" id="4a8f096a-854a-4e5f-a42a-ce92f4bdefd6-link">4</a></sup> passes, digital keys,<sup data-fn="19c438b6-84ce-40cc-a84c-aa5337dcec81" class="fn"><a href="#19c438b6-84ce-40cc-a84c-aa5337dcec81" id="19c438b6-84ce-40cc-a84c-aa5337dcec81-link">5</a></sup> and more. <a href="https://www.samsung.com/us/support/owners/app/samsung-wallet?r=true&referrer=usnewsroom" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Samsung Wallet</a> is designed to keep what users need within reach while supporting an open ecosystem through trusted partner integrations that expand user experiences over time. Plus, enjoy added peace of mind with Samsung’s on-device defense-grade security platform Samsung Knox.</p>



<p>“Partnering with Samsung is a strong example of Barclays partner-first strategy and the innovation we’re bringing to the market with some of the world’s most recognized brands,” said Denny Nealon, CEO of Barclays US Consumer Bank. “Together, we’re creating seamless, rewarding digital banking capabilities and products that meet customers where they are — in their <a href="https://www.samsung.com/us/support/owners/app/samsung-wallet?r=true&referrer=usnewsroom" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Samsung Wallet</a> — and fit naturally into how they live, shop, and connect.”</p>



<p>“Consumers expect payments to be embedded into the digital experiences they use every day,” said Kirk Stuart, SVP, Head of North America Enablers, Merchants and Fintechs at Visa. “By bringing together Samsung’s innovation, Barclays’ co-brand capabilities, and Visa’s trusted network, Samsung Galaxy Card delivers a connected experience that helps consumers earn rewards while providing the security and acceptance needed to support commerce wherever they choose to shop.”</p>



<p>Eligible early access customers can apply online for Samsung Galaxy Card today. <a href="https://www.barclaycardus.com/applycontent/TnCs.jsp?tc48355" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Terms apply.</a> General public applications begin July 22, 2026.</p>
